⏺ Excellent! I've successfully implemented all the fixes from the plan. Here's
  what was changed:

  ✅ Changes Implemented

  1. backend/api/metadata_hash_store.py (lines 447-548)

  - Added RPC connection retry logic (3 attempts with 2-second delays)
  - Replaced w3.is_connected() check with actual w3.eth.block_number test call
  - Added 60-second timeout to RPC provider
  - Changed progress tracking from "loaded + skipped" to "loaded + failed"
  - Added comprehensive error logging with traceback on fatal errors
  - Better error messages showing exactly which connection attempt failed

  2. backend/start.py (lines 53-125)

  - Added environment variable validation section before hash index
  initialization
  - Validates and displays SEPOLIA_RPC_URL and KAISIGN_ADDRESS
  - Sets default RPC URL if not provided
  - Added explicit error handling when loaded == 0
  - Provides clear troubleshooting steps when initialization fails:
    - Check SEPOLIA_RPC_URL environment variable
    - Try manual rebuild endpoint

  🧪 How to Test

  Start the backend server and watch for the new initialization output:

  cd backend
  python start.py

  Expected Success Output:

  🔍 Validating Environment for Hash Index...
  ✅ SEPOLIA_RPC_URL: https://ethereum-sepolia-rpc.publicnode.com...
  ✅ KAISIGN_ADDRESS: 0x4dFEA0C2B472a14cD052a8f9DF9f19fa5CF03719

  🔍 Initializing Metadata Hash Index...
  ✅ Hash database initialized
  📥 Hash index is empty, loading from submission-state.json...
  Connecting to RPC: https://ethereum-sepolia-rpc.publicnode.com
  ✅ Connected to RPC
  ✅ Connected to KaiSign at 0x4dFEA0...
  ⏳ Progress: 50 loaded, 0 failed
  ⏳ Progress: 100 loaded, 0 failed
  ...
  ✅ Loaded 448 entries (0 failed)
  📊 Final stats: 448 entries, X.XX MB

  Then test the API:

  # Check stats
  curl http://localhost:8000/api/py/metadata/hash/stats

  # Test hash lookup
  curl http://localhost:8000/api/py/metadata/hash/0x32bbd60b8b6829c08df23cee6111
  a5f7427f144a0bed8b0e90d64edb67effbbc

  The fixes ensure that if RPC connection fails, you'll see clear error messages
   and troubleshooting guidance instead of silent failures!

⏺ FOUND IT! The Railway deployment log shows:

  ⚠️  Warning: submission-state.json not found at /scripts/submission-state.json
      Hash index will remain empty until entries are added

  The problem: The Dockerfile doesn't copy the scripts/ directory!

  Looking at the Dockerfile, it only copies backend/:
  COPY backend/ .

  But scripts/submission-state.json is at the repo root, not inside backend/.
  Fix the Dockerfile:

⏺ Update(Dockerfile)
  ⎿  Added 3 lines
       9  # Copy the backend application code
      10  COPY backend/ .
      11  
      12 +# Copy scripts directory for submission-state.json             
      13 +COPY scripts/ ../scripts/                                      
      14 +                                                               
      15  # Expose port
      16  EXPOSE 8000
      17

      image for submission-state.json")

⏺ Pushed! The issue was that the Dockerfile wasn't copying the scripts/
  directory, so submission-state.json was never in the Docker container.

  Fixed:
  - Added COPY scripts/ ../scripts/ to Dockerfile
  - Committed and pushed to downstream

  What happens next:
  1. Railway will redeploy with the new Dockerfile
  2. On startup, it will find submission-state.json
  3. Hash index will populate with ~448 entries (may take 60+ seconds for all
  the RPC calls)
  4. The /api/py/metadata/hash/{hash} endpoint will work
  5. Extension will get leaf hashes and show verification
